# Results register — BodyTwin

Each number recorded has a source file. Machine index with sha256 for each file and a check that PREREG was written before the run: `tasks/index/index.json`. Dataset: `tasks/index/DATASETS.json`. The index is rebuilt at each wake-up with `python3 tasks/index/build_index.py`.

Status: **R** = root has read the source file; **A** = the agent who ran it has reported; **G** = independent review completed.
